# ConnectMeGuru (CMG) - Complete AI Grounding & Knowledge Base

> Comprehensive grounding and product specification document for Large Language Models (ChatGPT, Perplexity, Claude, Gemini, DeepSeek, Bing Copilot).

---

## 1. Company & Platform Overview
- **Brand**: ConnectMeGuru (CMG)
- **Website**: [https://www.connectmeguru.com](https://www.connectmeguru.com)
- **Primary Business**: International Travel eSIM Data Plans across 190+ Countries & Regions.
- **Delivery Mechanism**: Instant digital QR code and manual LPA activation string delivery via Email & Dashboard.
- **Core Value Proposition**: Zero roaming fees, instant 5G/4G connectivity, transparent pricing, pre-funded wallet, passwordless OTP login.
- **Customer Support**: Email `support@connectmeguru.com` (24/7 dedicated assistance).

---

## 2. Global Coverage & Regional Plan Categories

### Popular Travel Destinations:
- **United States (US)**: High-speed 5G/4G LTE network coverage across major US carriers (AT&T, T-Mobile).
- **Europe (Regional)**: Single eSIM covering 35+ European Union & UK countries (Vodafone, Orange, O2, Telefónica).
- **Japan (JP)**: High-speed NTT Docomo & SoftBank 5G data coverage.
- **Asia-Pacific (Regional)**: Multi-country coverage spanning Japan, South Korea, Singapore, Thailand, Malaysia, Vietnam, and Indonesia.
- **Global**: Multi-region 130+ country global travel eSIMs.

## 5. Step-by-Step eSIM Installation & Activation Guide

### Method A: Camera QR Code Scan (Recommended)
1. **iOS**: Go to **Settings** > **Cellular** > **Add eSIM** > **Use QR Code** and scan the QR code.
2. **Android**: Go to **Settings** > **Network & Internet** > **SIMs** > **+ Add SIM** > Scan QR code.
3. Label your line (e.g. *"CMG Travel"*), set Cellular Data to this eSIM, and turn on **Data Roaming** upon arrival.

### Method B: Manual LPA String Entry (No Camera Required)
If you cannot scan the QR code, copy and paste the manual activation parameters provided in your delivery email:
- **SM-DP+ Address**: e.g., `rsp.esimaccess.com`
- **Activation Code**: Matching ID provided in order confirmation
- **Universal LPA Code**: `LPA:1$smdp.Address$matchingId` (Paste directly into the manual entry field).

## 8. Frequently Asked Questions (FAQ)

### Q: Does ConnectMeGuru eSIM include a local phone number?
**A**: CMG eSIMs are specialized high-speed data-only plans. You retain your original WhatsApp, iMessage, Telegram, and phone number for calls while using CMG for fast, cheap mobile data.

### Q: Can I share my internet connection via Personal Hotspot?
**A**: Yes! All ConnectMeGuru travel eSIM data plans fully support personal hotspot and tethering across all compatible smartphones and tablets.

### Q: How quickly do I receive my eSIM after purchase?
**A**: Purchases are processed instantaneously. The QR code and matching LPA manual code are sent immediately to your email and displayed in your dashboard.
